```bash
cat > src/features/hitl/components/TaskStats.tsx << 'EOF'
import { useQuery } from '@tanstack/react-query';
import { hitlApi } from '../../../shared/api/batuta';
import { CheckCircle, Clock, AlertCircle, Activity } from 'lucide-react';

export function TaskStats() {
  const { data: stats, isLoading } = useQuery({
    queryKey: ['task-stats'],
    queryFn: () => hitlApi.getStats(),
    refetchInterval: 10000,
  });

  if (isLoading) {
    return (
      <div className="grid grid-cols-4 gap-4">
        {[...Array(4)].map((_, i) => (
          <div key={i} className="bg-uch-secondary/20 rounded-lg p-4 animate-pulse">
            <div className="h-4 bg-uch-secondary/40 rounded w-20 mb-2" />
            <div className="h-8 bg-uch-secondary/40 rounded w-12" />
          </div>
        ))}
      </div>
    );
  }

  const statItems = [
    {
      label: 'Ready for Review',
      value: stats?.ready_for_review || 0,
      icon: <AlertCircle className="text-yellow-500" size={20} />,
      color: 'text-yellow-500',
    },
    {
      label: 'Pending',
      value: stats?.pending || 0,
      icon: <Clock className="text-blue-500" size={20} />,
      color: 'text-blue-500',
    },
    {
      label: 'Completed',
      value: stats?.completed || 0,
      icon: <CheckCircle className="text-green-500" size={20} />,
      color: 'text-green-500',
    },
    {
      label: 'Total Tasks',
      value: stats?.total || 0,
      icon: <Activity className="text-purple-500" size={20} />,
      color: 'text-purple-500',
    },
  ];

  return (
    <div className="grid grid-cols-4 gap-4">
      {statItems.map((item) => (
        <div key={item.label} className="bg-uch-secondary/30 rounded-lg p-4 border border-uch-secondary">
          <div className="flex items-center justify-between">
            <span className="text-uch-text-secondary text-sm">{item.label}</span>
            {item.icon}
          </div>
          <div className={`text-2xl font-bold mt-2 ${item.color}`}>
            {item.value}
          </div>
        </div>
      ))}
    </div>
  );
}
EOF

cat > src/features/hitl/components/CreateTaskForm.tsx << 'EOF'
import { useState } from 'react';
import { useMutation, useQueryClient } from '@tanstack/react-query';
import { hitlApi } from '../../../shared/api/batuta';
import { Send } from 'lucide-react';
import toast from 'react-hot-toast';

interface CreateTaskFormProps {
  onSuccess?: () => void;
}

export function CreateTaskForm({ onSuccess }: CreateTaskFormProps) {
  const [title, setTitle] = useState('');
  const [description, setDescription] = useState('');
  const [agentType, setAgentType] = useState('codecraft');
  const queryClient = useQueryClient();

  const createMutation = useMutation({
    mutationFn: (data: any) => hitlApi.createTask(data),
    onSuccess: () => {
      queryClient.invalidateQueries({ queryKey: ['hitl-tasks'] });
      toast.success('Task created successfully');
      setTitle('');
      setDescription('');
      onSuccess?.();
    },
    onError: (error) => {
      toast.error(`Failed to create task: ${error.message}`);
    },
  });

  const handleSubmit = (e: React.FormEvent) => {
    e.preventDefault();
    if (!title.trim() || !description.trim()) {
      toast.error('Please fill in all fields');
      return;
    }

    createMutation.mutate({
      title,
      description,
      data: { agent_id: agentType },
      status: 'pending',
    });
  };

  return (
    <form onSubmit={handleSubmit} className="max-w-2xl bg-uch-secondary/20 rounded-lg p-6 border border-uch-secondary">
      <div className="mb-4">
        <label className="block text-sm font-medium text-uch-text-secondary mb-2">
          Task Title
        </label>
        <input
          type="text"
          value={title}
          onChange={(e) => setTitle(e.target.value)}
          placeholder="e.g., Create monitoring configuration"
          className="w-full px-3 py-2 bg-uch-primary border border-uch-secondary rounded-md text-uch-text placeholder-uch-text-secondary/50 focus:outline-none focus:border-uch-accent"
        />
      </div>

      <div className="mb-4">
        <label className="block text-sm font-medium text-uch-text-secondary mb-2">
          Description
        </label>
        <textarea
          value={description}
          onChange={(e) => setDescription(e.target.value)}
          placeholder="Describe what you want the agent to do..."
          rows={4}
          className="w-full px-3 py-2 bg-uch-primary border border-uch-secondary rounded-md text-uch-text placeholder-uch-text-secondary/50 focus:outline-none focus:border-uch-accent resize-none"
        />
      </div>

      <div className="mb-6">
        <label className="block text-sm font-medium text-uch-text-secondary mb-2">
          Agent Type
        </label>
        <select
          value={agentType}
          onChange={(e) => setAgentType(e.target.value)}
          className="w-full px-3 py-2 bg-uch-primary border border-uch-secondary rounded-md text-uch-text focus:outline-none focus:border-uch-accent"
        >
          <option value="codecraft">CodeCraft (Code Generation)</option>
          <option value="explorer">Explorer (Code Analysis)</option>
          <option value="architect">Architect (Design)</option>
          <option value="general">General</option>
        </select>
      </div>

      <button
        type="submit"
        disabled={createMutation.isPending}
        className="btn-primary flex items-center gap-2 disabled:opacity-50"
      >
        <Send size={16} />
        {createMutation.isPending ? 'Creating...' : 'Create Task'}
      </button>
    </form>
  );
}
EOF

# 7. Запускаем снова
npm run dev
```

```bash
cat > src/App.tsx << 'EOF'
import { QueryClient, QueryClientProvider } from '@tanstack/react-query';
import { Toaster } from 'react-hot-toast';
import { HITLCenter } from './features/hitl/components/HITLCenter';
import { useState } from 'react';
import { Activity, History, Network, Bot } from 'lucide-react';

const queryClient = new QueryClient({
  defaultOptions: {
    queries: {
      refetchOnWindowFocus: false,
      retry: 1,
      staleTime: 5000,
    },
  },
});

function App() {
  const [activeTab, setActiveTab] = useState<'hitl' | 'history' | 'agents' | 'graph'>('hitl');

  return (
    <QueryClientProvider client={queryClient}>
      <div className="min-h-screen bg-uch-primary">
        {/* Header */}
        <header className="border-b border-uch-secondary bg-uch-primary/95 sticky top-0 z-50">
          <div className="container mx-auto px-4 py-3">
            <div className="flex items-center justify-between">
              <div className="flex items-center gap-2">
                <div className="w-8 h-8 bg-uch-accent rounded flex items-center justify-center">
                  <span className="text-black font-bold text-xl">U</span>
                </div>
                <span className="text-uch-accent font-bold text-xl">Tactical Command</span>
                <span className="text-uch-text-secondary text-sm ml-2">v3.0</span>
              </div>
              
              <div className="flex items-center gap-2">
                <div className="flex items-center gap-1">
                  <div className="w-2 h-2 rounded-full bg-green-500 animate-pulse" />
                  <span className="text-xs text-uch-text-secondary">Batuta</span>
                </div>
              </div>
            </div>
          </div>
        </header>

        {/* Navigation */}
        <nav className="border-b border-uch-secondary">
          <div className="container mx-auto px-4">
            <div className="flex gap-6">
              <TabButton 
                active={activeTab === 'hitl'} 
                onClick={() => setActiveTab('hitl')}
                icon={<Activity size={18} />}
                label="HITL Center"
              />
              <TabButton 
                active={activeTab === 'history'} 
                onClick={() => setActiveTab('history')}
                icon={<History size={18} />}
                label="History"
              />
              <TabButton 
                active={activeTab === 'agents'} 
                onClick={() => setActiveTab('agents')}
                icon={<Bot size={18} />}
                label="Agents"
              />
              <TabButton 
                active={activeTab === 'graph'} 
                onClick={() => setActiveTab('graph')}
                icon={<Network size={18} />}
                label="Graph"
              />
            </div>
          </div>
        </nav>

        {/* Main content */}
        <main className="container mx-auto px-4 py-6">
          {activeTab === 'hitl' && <HITLCenter />}
          {activeTab === 'history' && (
            <div className="text-center py-12 text-uch-text-secondary">
              History page (coming soon)
            </div>
          )}
          {activeTab === 'agents' && (
            <div className="text-center py-12 text-uch-text-secondary">
              Agents page (coming soon)
            </div>
          )}
          {activeTab === 'graph' && (
            <div className="text-center py-12 text-uch-text-secondary">
              Graph page (coming soon)
            </div>
          )}
        </main>
      </div>
      
      <Toaster 
        position="top-right"
        toastOptions={{
          style: {
            background: '#1e2a3a',
            color: '#e2e8f0',
            border: '1px solid #334155',
          },
          success: {
            iconTheme: {
              primary: '#00ff9d',
              secondary: '#000',
            },
          },
        }}
      />
    </QueryClientProvider>
  );
}

function TabButton({ 
  active, 
  onClick, 
  icon, 
  label 
}: { 
  active: boolean; 
  onClick: () => void; 
  icon: React.ReactNode; 
  label: string;
}) {
  return (
    <button
      onClick={onClick}
      className={`flex items-center gap-2 px-3 py-3 transition-colors border-b-2 ${
        active 
          ? 'text-uch-accent border-uch-accent' 
          : 'text-uch-text-secondary border-transparent hover:text-uch-text'
      }`}
    >
      {icon}
      <span className="text-sm font-medium">{label}</span>
    </button>
  );
}

export default App;
EOF

# 3. Обновить main.tsx (убрать StrictMode временно для теста)
```

```bash
    border: none;
    border-radius: 6px;
    padding: 0.5rem 1rem;
    cursor: pointer;
    transition: background-color 0.2s;
  }
  
  .btn-primary:hover {
    background-color: #00cc7d;
  }
  
  .btn-secondary {
    background-color: #1e2a3a;
    color: #e2e8f0;
    border: 1px solid #334155;
    border-radius: 6px;
    padding: 0.5rem 1rem;
    cursor: pointer;
    transition: background-color 0.2s;
  }
  
  .btn-secondary:hover {
    background-color: #2a3a4a;
  }
}
```

```bash
export type TaskStatus = 'pending' | 'ready_for_review' | 'approved' | 'completed' | 'failed' | 'integrating';
export type AgentType = 'codecraft' | 'integrator' | 'explorer' | 'architect' | 'general' | 'clipping' | 'search' | 'graph';

export interface HITLTask {
  id: string;
  title: string;
  description: string;
  status: TaskStatus;
  assigned_to: AgentType;
  created_at: string;
  approved_at?: string;
  completed_at?: string;
  data?: Record<string, any>;
  result?: {
    code?: string;
    config?: Record<string, any>;
    analysis?: Record<string, any>;
    patch?: string;
  };
  error?: string;
}

export interface TaskFilter {
  status?: TaskStatus;
  agent?: AgentType;
  dateFrom?: string;
  dateTo?: string;
  search?: string;
}

export interface TaskStats {
  total: number;
  pending: number;
  ready_for_review: number;
  approved: number;
  completed: number;
  failed: number;
}
EOF

# 3. Проверим API клиент
cat src/shared/api/batuta.ts | head -30

# 4. Если API клиент не создан или есть ошибки — пересоздадим
cat > src/shared/api/batuta.ts << 'EOF'
import axios from 'axios';
import type { HITLTask, TaskStats } from '../types/hitl';

const batutaApi = axios.create({
  baseURL: '/api/batuta',
  timeout: 10000,
  headers: {
    'Content-Type': 'application/json',
  },
});

// HITL Tasks API
export const hitlApi = {
  // Получить все задачи
  getTasks: async (status?: string): Promise<HITLTask[]> => {
    const params = status ? { status } : undefined;
    const response = await batutaApi.get('/api/v1/hitl/tasks', { params });
    return response.data;
  },

  // Получить задачу по ID
  getTask: async (taskId: string): Promise<HITLTask> => {
    const response = await batutaApi.get(`/api/v1/hitl/tasks/${taskId}`);
    return response.data;
  },

  // Создать задачу
  createTask: async (task: Partial<HITLTask>): Promise<HITLTask> => {
    const response = await batutaApi.post('/api/v1/hitl/tasks', task);
    return response.data;
  },

  // Одобрить задачу
  approveTask: async (taskId: string, approvedBy: string = 'operator'): Promise<HITLTask> => {
    const response = await batutaApi.post(`/api/v1/hitl/tasks/${taskId}/approve`, {
      approved_by: approvedBy,
    });
    return response.data;
  },

  // Получить историю
  getHistory: async (limit: number = 100, status?: string): Promise<HITLTask[]> => {
    const params: Record<string, any> = { limit };
    if (status) params.status = status;
    const response = await batutaApi.get('/api/v1/hitl/history', { params });
    return response.data;
  },

  // Получить статистику
  getStats: async (): Promise<TaskStats> => {
    const response = await batutaApi.get('/api/v1/hitl/stats');
    return response.data;
  },
};

// Agents API
export const agentsApi = {
  getAgents: async () => {
    const response = await batutaApi.get('/api/v1/agents');
    return response.data;
  },

  getServices: async () => {
    const response = await batutaApi.get('/api/v1/services');
    return response.data;
  },
};

// Health check
export const healthApi = {
  check: async () => {
    const response = await batutaApi.get('/health');
    return response.data;
  },
};

export default batutaApi;
EOF
```

## 🔧 КОМАНДА 13: ОСВОБОЖДЕНИЕ ПОРТА 8010

**Исполнитель:** Вы

**Задача:** Найти процесс на порту 8010 и остановить его

**Действия:**

```bash
# 1. Найти процесс на порту 8010
lsof -i :8010

# 2. Убить процесс (замените PID на тот что покажет команда выше)
# Например если PID 99746:

# 3. Проверить что порт свободен
lsof -i :8010

# 4. Запустить Batuta снова
python main.py
```

**Если не хотите убивать процесс, можно запустить Batuta на другом порту:**

```bash
# Запустить на порту 8015
uvicorn main:app --host 0.0.0.0 --port 8015 --reload
```

Но тогда нужно обновить прокси в `vite.config.ts`:

```bash
cd /Users/kirillkravcov/UniversalCreativeHub/tactical-command-v3

# Обновить порт в конфиге
sed -i '' 's/8010/8015/g' vite.config.ts
```

```bash
import { useState } from 'react';
import { Check, X, ChevronDown, ChevronUp, Code, FileText, Clock } from 'lucide-react';
import { formatDistanceToNow } from 'date-fns';

interface HITLTask {
  id: string;
  title: string;
  description: string;
  status: string;
  assigned_to: string;
  created_at: string;
  result?: {
    code?: string;
    config?: Record<string, any>;
    analysis?: Record<string, any>;
  };
}

interface TaskCardProps {
  task: HITLTask;
  onApprove: (taskId: string) => void;
  isApproving: boolean;
}

export function TaskCard({ task, onApprove, isApproving }: TaskCardProps) {
  const [isExpanded, setIsExpanded] = useState(false);

  const timeAgo = formatDistanceToNow(new Date(task.created_at), { addSuffix: true });

  return (
    <div className="task-card">
      {/* Header */}
      <div className="flex items-start justify-between">
        <div className="flex-1">
          <div className="flex items-center gap-3 mb-2">
            <h3 className="task-title">{task.title}</h3>
            <span className={`status-badge status-${task.status.replace(/_/g, '-')}`}>
              {task.status.toUpperCase().replace(/_/g, ' ')}
            </span>
          </div>
          <p className="text-uch-text-secondary text-sm mb-2">{task.description}</p>
          <div className="flex items-center gap-4 text-xs text-uch-text-secondary/70">
            <span className="flex items-center gap-1">
              <Clock size={12} />
              {timeAgo}
            </span>
            <span>ID: {task.id}</span>
            <span>Agent: {task.assigned_to}</span>
          </div>
        </div>

        {/* Actions */}
        <div className="flex items-center gap-2">
          <button
            onClick={() => setIsExpanded(!isExpanded)}
            className="p-2 text-uch-text-secondary hover:text-uch-text transition-colors"
          >
            {isExpanded ? <ChevronUp size={18} /> : <ChevronDown size={18} />}
          </button>
        </div>
      </div>

      {/* Expanded content */}
      {isExpanded && (
        <div className="mt-4 pt-4 border-t border-uch-secondary">
          {/* Preview of result */}
          {task.result && (
            <div className="mb-4">
              <div className="flex items-center gap-2 text-sm text-uch-text-secondary mb-2">
                {task.result.code ? <Code size={14} /> : <FileText size={14} />}
                <span>Generated Content Preview</span>
              </div>
              <pre className="bg-uch-secondary/50 p-3 rounded text-xs overflow-x-auto max-h-64">
                {task.result.code || JSON.stringify(task.result.config || task.result.analysis, null, 2)}
              </pre>
            </div>
          )}

          {/* Action buttons */}
          <div className="flex gap-2">
            <button
              onClick={() => onApprove(task.id)}
              disabled={isApproving}
              className="btn-primary flex items-center gap-2 disabled:opacity-50"
            >
              <Check size={16} />
              {isApproving ? 'Approving...' : 'Approve & Execute'}
            </button>
            <button className="btn-secondary flex items-center gap-2">
              <X size={16} />
              Reject
            </button>
          </div>
        </div>
      )}
    </div>
  );
}
EOF

# 4. Обновим HITLCenter - уберем импорт типа
cat > src/features/hitl/components/HITLCenter.tsx << 'EOF'
import { useQuery, useMutation, useQueryClient } from '@tanstack/react-query';
import { hitlApi } from '../../../shared/api/batuta';
import { TaskCard } from './TaskCard';
import { CreateTaskForm } from './CreateTaskForm';
import { TaskStats } from './TaskStats';
import { useState } from 'react';
import { RefreshCw, Plus, List } from 'lucide-react';
import toast from 'react-hot-toast';

export function HITLCenter() {
  const [activeTab, setActiveTab] = useState<'active' | 'create'>('active');
  const queryClient = useQueryClient();

  const { data: tasks, isLoading, refetch } = useQuery({
    queryKey: ['hitl-tasks'],
    queryFn: () => hitlApi.getTasks(),
    refetchInterval: 5000,
  });

  const approveMutation = useMutation({
    mutationFn: (taskId: string) => hitlApi.approveTask(taskId),
    onSuccess: () => {
      queryClient.invalidateQueries({ queryKey: ['hitl-tasks'] });
      toast.success('Task approved and sent to execution');
    },
    onError: (error: any) => {
      toast.error(`Failed to approve: ${error.message}`);
    },
  });

  const handleApprove = (taskId: string) => {
    approveMutation.mutate(taskId);
  };

  const handleRefresh = () => {
    refetch();
    toast.success('Tasks refreshed');
  };

  const readyTasks = tasks?.filter(t => t.status === 'ready_for_review') || [];

  return (
    <div>
      <div className="mb-6">
        <div className="flex items-center justify-between mb-4">
          <h1 className="text-2xl font-bold text-uch-text">HITL Center</h1>
          <div className="flex gap-2">
            <button onClick={handleRefresh} className="btn-secondary flex items-center gap-2">
              <RefreshCw size={16} />
              Refresh
            </button>
          </div>
        </div>
        <TaskStats />
      </div>

      <div className="border-b border-uch-secondary mb-6">
        <div className="flex gap-4">
          <button
            onClick={() => setActiveTab('active')}
            className={`flex items-center gap-2 px-4 py-2 font-medium transition-colors ${
              activeTab === 'active'
                ? 'text-uch-accent border-b-2 border-uch-accent'
                : 'text-uch-text-secondary hover:text-uch-text'
            }`}
          >
            <List size={18} />
            Active Tasks
            {readyTasks.length > 0 && (
              <span className="ml-2 px-2 py-0.5 bg-uch-accent/20 text-uch-accent rounded-full text-xs">
                {readyTasks.length}
              </span>
            )}
          </button>
          <button
            onClick={() => setActiveTab('create')}
            className={`flex items-center gap-2 px-4 py-2 font-medium transition-colors ${
              activeTab === 'create'
                ? 'text-uch-accent border-b-2 border-uch-accent'
                : 'text-uch-text-secondary hover:text-uch-text'
            }`}
          >
            <Plus size={18} />
            Create Task
          </button>
        </div>
      </div>

      {activeTab === 'active' ? (
        <div>
          {isLoading ? (
            <div className="text-center py-12">
              <div className="inline-block animate-spin rounded-full h-8 w-8 border-2 border-uch-accent border-t-transparent" />
              <p className="mt-4 text-uch-text-secondary">Loading tasks...</p>
            </div>
          ) : readyTasks.length > 0 ? (
            <div className="space-y-3">
              {readyTasks.map((task: any) => (
                <TaskCard
                  key={task.id}
                  task={task}
                  onApprove={handleApprove}
                  isApproving={approveMutation.isPending}
                />
              ))}
            </div>
          ) : (
            <div className="text-center py-12 bg-uch-secondary/20 rounded-lg">
              <List size={48} className="mx-auto text-uch-text-secondary mb-3" />
              <p className="text-uch-text-secondary">No tasks ready for review</p>
              <p className="text-sm text-uch-text-secondary/70 mt-1">
                Create a new task or wait for incoming tasks
              </p>
            </div>
          )}
        </div>
      ) : (
        <CreateTaskForm onSuccess={() => setActiveTab('active')} />
      )}
    </div>
  );
}
EOF
```

## 🔧 КОМАНДА 19: ИСПРАВЛЕНИЕ ПРОКСИ И API КЛИЕНТА

**Исполнитель:** Вы

**Задача:** Исправить дублирование `/api/batuta` в URL

**Действия:**

```bash
cd /Users/kirillkravcov/UniversalCreativeHub/tactical-command-v3

# 1. Исправим API клиент - уберем /api/v1 из путей
cat > src/shared/api/batuta.ts << 'EOF'
import axios from 'axios';

const batutaApi = axios.create({
  baseURL: '/api/batuta',
  timeout: 10000,
  headers: {
    'Content-Type': 'application/json',
  },
});

// HITL Tasks API
export const hitlApi = {
  // Получить все задачи
  getTasks: async (status?: string) => {
    const params = status ? { status } : undefined;
    const response = await batutaApi.get('/api/v1/hitl/tasks', { params });
    return response.data;
  },

  // Получить задачу по ID
  getTask: async (taskId: string) => {
    const response = await batutaApi.get(`/api/v1/hitl/tasks/${taskId}`);
    return response.data;
  },

  // Создать задачу
  createTask: async (task: any) => {
    const response = await batutaApi.post('/api/v1/hitl/tasks', task);
    return response.data;
  },

  // Одобрить задачу
  approveTask: async (taskId: string, approvedBy: string = 'operator') => {
    const response = await batutaApi.post(`/api/v1/hitl/tasks/${taskId}/approve`, {
      approved_by: approvedBy,
    });
    return response.data;
  },

  // Получить историю
  getHistory: async (limit: number = 100, status?: string) => {
    const params: Record<string, any> = { limit };
    if (status) params.status = status;
    const response = await batutaApi.get('/api/v1/hitl/history', { params });
    return response.data;
  },

  // Получить статистику
  getStats: async () => {
    const response = await batutaApi.get('/api/v1/hitl/stats');
    return response.data;
  },
};

// Agents API
export const agentsApi = {
  getAgents: async () => {
    const response = await batutaApi.get('/api/v1/agents');
    return response.data;
  },

  getServices: async () => {
    const response = await batutaApi.get('/api/v1/services');
    return response.data;
  },
};

export default batutaApi;
EOF

# 2. Исправим CreateTaskForm - добавим правильные поля
cat > src/features/hitl/components/CreateTaskForm.tsx << 'EOF'
import { useState } from 'react';
import { useMutation, useQueryClient } from '@tanstack/react-query';
import { hitlApi } from '../../../shared/api/batuta';
import { Send } from 'lucide-react';
import toast from 'react-hot-toast';

interface CreateTaskFormProps {
  onSuccess?: () => void;
}

export function CreateTaskForm({ onSuccess }: CreateTaskFormProps) {
  const [title, setTitle] = useState('');
  const [description, setDescription] = useState('');
  const [agentType, setAgentType] = useState('codecraft');
  const queryClient = useQueryClient();

  const createMutation = useMutation({
    mutationFn: (data: any) => hitlApi.createTask(data),
    onSuccess: () => {
      queryClient.invalidateQueries({ queryKey: ['hitl-tasks'] });
      toast.success('Task created successfully');
      setTitle('');
      setDescription('');
      onSuccess?.();
    },
    onError: (error: any) => {
      toast.error(`Failed to create task: ${error.message}`);
    },
  });

  const handleSubmit = (e: React.FormEvent) => {
    e.preventDefault();
    if (!title.trim() || !description.trim()) {
      toast.error('Please fill in all fields');
      return;
    }

    createMutation.mutate({
      title,
      description,
      status: 'pending',
      assigned_to: agentType,
      data: { agent_id: agentType }
    });
  };

  return (
    <form onSubmit={handleSubmit} className="max-w-2xl bg-uch-secondary/20 rounded-lg p-6 border border-uch-secondary">
      <div className="mb-4">
        <label className="block text-sm font-medium text-uch-text-secondary mb-2">
          Task Title
        </label>
        <input
          type="text"
          value={title}
          onChange={(e) => setTitle(e.target.value)}
          placeholder="e.g., Create monitoring configuration"
          className="w-full px-3 py-2 bg-uch-primary border border-uch-secondary rounded-md text-uch-text placeholder-uch-text-secondary/50 focus:outline-none focus:border-uch-accent"
        />
      </div>

      <div className="mb-4">
        <label className="block text-sm font-medium text-uch-text-secondary mb-2">
          Description
        </label>
        <textarea
          value={description}
          onChange={(e) => setDescription(e.target.value)}
          placeholder="Describe what you want the agent to do..."
          rows={4}
          className="w-full px-3 py-2 bg-uch-primary border border-uch-secondary rounded-md text-uch-text placeholder-uch-text-secondary/50 focus:outline-none focus:border-uch-accent resize-none"
        />
      </div>

      <div className="mb-6">
        <label className="block text-sm font-medium text-uch-text-secondary mb-2">
          Agent Type
        </label>
        <select
          value={agentType}
          onChange={(e) => setAgentType(e.target.value)}
          className="w-full px-3 py-2 bg-uch-primary border border-uch-secondary rounded-md text-uch-text focus:outline-none focus:border-uch-accent"
        >
          <option value="codecraft">CodeCraft (Code Generation)</option>
          <option value="explorer">Explorer (Code Analysis)</option>
          <option value="architect">Architect (Design)</option>
          <option value="general">General</option>
        </select>
      </div>

      <button
        type="submit"
        disabled={createMutation.isPending}
        className="btn-primary flex items-center gap-2 disabled:opacity-50"
      >
        <Send size={16} />
        {createMutation.isPending ? 'Creating...' : 'Create Task'}
      </button>
    </form>
  );
}
EOF
```

```bash
cat > src/features/hitl/components/CreateTaskForm.tsx << 'EOF'
import { useState } from 'react';
import { useMutation, useQueryClient } from '@tanstack/react-query';
import { hitlApi } from '../../../shared/api/batuta';
import { Send } from 'lucide-react';
import toast from 'react-hot-toast';

interface CreateTaskFormProps {
  onSuccess?: () => void;
}

export function CreateTaskForm({ onSuccess }: CreateTaskFormProps) {
  const [title, setTitle] = useState('');
  const [description, setDescription] = useState('');
  const [agentType, setAgentType] = useState('codecraft');
  const queryClient = useQueryClient();

  const createMutation = useMutation({
    mutationFn: (data: any) => hitlApi.createTask(data),
    onSuccess: () => {
      queryClient.invalidateQueries({ queryKey: ['hitl-tasks'] });
      toast.success('Task created successfully');
      setTitle('');
      setDescription('');
      onSuccess?.();
    },
    onError: (error: any) => {
      console.error('Create task error:', error);
      toast.error(`Failed to create task: ${error.message}`);
    },
  });

  const handleSubmit = (e: React.FormEvent) => {
    e.preventDefault();
    if (!title.trim() || !description.trim()) {
      toast.error('Please fill in all fields');
      return;
    }

    // Batuta ожидает id и created_at, но сервер сам их генерирует
    // Проблема в том что Pydantic требует их при валидации
    createMutation.mutate({
      id: `temp_${Date.now()}`,  // Временный id, сервер перезапишет
      title,
      description,
      status: 'pending',
      assigned_to: agentType,
      created_at: new Date().toISOString(),
      data: { agent_id: agentType }
    });
  };

  return (
    <form onSubmit={handleSubmit} className="max-w-2xl bg-uch-secondary/20 rounded-lg p-6 border border-uch-secondary">
      <div className="mb-4">
        <label className="block text-sm font-medium text-uch-text-secondary mb-2">
          Task Title
        </label>
        <input
          type="text"
          value={title}
          onChange={(e) => setTitle(e.target.value)}
          placeholder="e.g., Create monitoring configuration"
          className="w-full px-3 py-2 bg-uch-primary border border-uch-secondary rounded-md text-uch-text placeholder-uch-text-secondary/50 focus:outline-none focus:border-uch-accent"
        />
      </div>

      <div className="mb-4">
        <label className="block text-sm font-medium text-uch-text-secondary mb-2">
          Description
        </label>
        <textarea
          value={description}
          onChange={(e) => setDescription(e.target.value)}
          placeholder="Describe what you want the agent to do..."
          rows={4}
          className="w-full px-3 py-2 bg-uch-primary border border-uch-secondary rounded-md text-uch-text placeholder-uch-text-secondary/50 focus:outline-none focus:border-uch-accent resize-none"
        />
      </div>

      <div className="mb-6">
        <label className="block text-sm font-medium text-uch-text-secondary mb-2">
          Agent Type
        </label>
        <select
          value={agentType}
          onChange={(e) => setAgentType(e.target.value)}
          className="w-full px-3 py-2 bg-uch-primary border border-uch-secondary rounded-md text-uch-text focus:outline-none focus:border-uch-accent"
        >
          <option value="codecraft">CodeCraft (Code Generation)</option>
          <option value="explorer">Explorer (Code Analysis)</option>
          <option value="architect">Architect (Design)</option>
          <option value="general">General</option>
        </select>
      </div>

      <button
        type="submit"
        disabled={createMutation.isPending}
        className="btn-primary flex items-center gap-2 disabled:opacity-50"
      >
        <Send size={16} />
        {createMutation.isPending ? 'Creating...' : 'Create Task'}
      </button>
    </form>
  );
}
EOF
```

## 📊 АНАЛИЗ ТЕКУЩЕЙ АРХИТЕКТУРЫ АГЕНТОВ

Судя по этапу 46, агенты должны работать так:

```
1. Clipping (Cmd+Shift+2) → создает задачу pending
2. NLP Router → определяет intent → назначает агента
3. Batuta → создает HITL задачу ready_for_review
4. Tactical Command → показывает задачу
5. Approve → вызывает CodeCraft → генерирует код
6. (Этап 47) Integrator → применяет изменения
```

**Вопросы для диагностики:**
1. Запущен ли Clipping API (порт 8011)?
2. Работает ли NLP Router?
3. CodeCraft агент активен?

#!/usr/bin/env python3
"""
Сборщик метрик производительности для Batuta
"""
import asyncio
import json
import time
from datetime import datetime
from pathlib import Path
import psutil
import httpx

class MetricsCollector:
    def __init__(self):
        self.metrics_dir = Path("/Users/kirillkravcov/UniversalCreativeHub/.center/metrics")
        self.metrics_dir.mkdir(exist_ok=True)
        
    async def collect_batuta_metrics(self):
        """Сбор метрик Batuta"""
        metrics = {
            "timestamp": datetime.now().isoformat(),
            "batuta": {},
            "system": {},
            "services": {}
        }
        
        # Batuta метрики
        try:
            async with httpx.AsyncClient() as client:
                # Проверка статуса
                resp = await client.get("http://localhost:8010/", timeout=2)
                metrics["batuta"]["status"] = resp.status_code == 200
                
                # Статистика событий
                resp = await client.get("http://localhost:8010/events/stats", timeout=2)
                if resp.status_code == 200:
                    events = resp.json()
                    metrics["batuta"]["events_total"] = events.get("total", 0)
                    metrics["batuta"]["events_24h"] = events.get("last_24h", 0)
        except:
            metrics["batuta"]["status"] = False
        
        # Системные метрики
        metrics["system"]["cpu_percent"] = psutil.cpu_percent(interval=1)
        metrics["system"]["memory_percent"] = psutil.virtual_memory().percent
        metrics["system"]["disk_usage"] = psutil.disk_usage('/').percent
        
        # Проверка сервисов
        services = {
            "search_api": 8003,
            "graph_api": 8004,
            "opencode": 52939,
            "node_red": 1880
        }
        
        for name, port in services.items():
            try:
                conn = psutil.net_connections()
                metrics["services"][name] = any(
                    c.laddr.port == port and c.status == 'LISTEN' 
                    for c in conn
                )
            except:
                metrics["services"][name] = False
        
        return metrics
    
    async def save_metrics(self):
        """Сохранение метрик"""
        metrics = await self.collect_batuta_metrics()
        
        # Сохраняем текущие
        with open(self.metrics_dir / "current.json", 'w') as f:
            json.dump(metrics, f, indent=2)
        
        # Добавляем в историю
        history_file = self.metrics_dir / "history.json"
        if history_file.exists():
            with open(history_file, 'r') as f:
                history = json.load(f)
        else:
            history = []
        
        history.append(metrics)
        if len(history) > 100:  # Храним последние 100 записей
            history = history[-100:]
        
        with open(history_file, 'w') as f:
            json.dump(history, f, indent=2)
        
        print(f"✅ Метрики сохранены: {metrics['timestamp']}")
    
    async def run(self):
        """Запуск сбора метрик"""
        while True:
            await self.save_metrics()
            await asyncio.sleep(300)  # Каждые 5 минут

if __name__ == "__main__":
    collector = MetricsCollector()

#!/usr/bin/env python3
"""
Автоматический ретригер для задач OpenCode
Запускается по расписанию и проверяет статус задач
"""
import asyncio
import json
import os
import sys
from datetime import datetime, timedelta
from pathlib import Path

sys.path.append(os.path.dirname(os.path.dirname(os.path.abspath(__file__))))
from tools.tool_opencode import OpenCodeTool

class OpenCodeRetryScheduler:
    def __init__(self):
        self.tool = OpenCodeTool()
        self.tasks_dir = Path("/Users/kirillkravcov/UniversalCreativeHub/.center/tasks")
        self.max_retries = 3
        
    async def check_pending_tasks(self):
        """Проверить задачи, ожидающие выполнения"""
        print(f"🔍 Проверка задач в {datetime.now()}")
        
        for task_file in self.tasks_dir.glob("*.json"):
            with open(task_file, 'r') as f:
                task = json.load(f)
            
            # Проверяем задачи в статусе processing или pending
            if task.get('status') in ['processing', 'pending']:
                await self.retry_task(task, task_file)
    
    async def retry_task(self, task: dict, task_file: Path):
        """Повторить выполнение задачи"""
        task_id = task.get('id')
        retries = task.get('retries', 0)
        
        if retries >= self.max_retries:
            print(f"❌ Задача {task_id} превысила лимит попыток")
            task['status'] = 'failed'
            task['error'] = 'Max retries exceeded'
        else:
            print(f"🔄 Повторная попытка {retries + 1} для задачи {task_id}")
            
            # Запускаем в OpenCode
            result = await self.tool.run_task(
                task.get('title', ''),
                task.get('context', {})
            )

## 🤖 **DeepSeek**

Понятно. В scheduler нет обработчика pending HITL задач — только метрики и OpenCode ретрай. 

**Это ключевой момент:** Batuta сейчас **НЕ ОБРАБАТЫВАЕТ** pending задачи автоматически. Задачи попадают в `ready_for_review` только через Clipping API (Cmd+Shift+2) или вручную.

## 📋 ИТОГИ ДИАГНОСТИКИ

### Что работает ✅
1. **React UI** запущен, стили работают
2. **Batuta API** работает, агенты зарегистрированы
3. **Создание задач** через UI работает (исправлено)
4. **Статистика** отображается корректно

### Что не работает ❌
1. **Автоматическая обработка pending задач** — нет воркера
2. **Clipping API** — не проверен (порт 8011)

### Что нужно для полного цикла
1. Запустить Clipping API (порт 8011)
2. Настроить NLP Router для автоматического создания ready_for_review задач
3. Или добавить в Batuta воркер для обработки pending

kirillkravcov@MacBook-Air batuta % cd /Users/kirillkravcov/UniversalCreativeHub/ai-farm/agents/batuta

# Вывести строки 205-280 (приблизительно где approve)

zsh: unknown file attribute: ^
    task = hitl_tasks.get(task_id)
    if not task:
        raise HTTPException(404, "Task not found")
    return task

@app.post("/api/v1/hitl/tasks/{task_id}/approve")
async def approve_hitl_task(task_id: str, approval: dict):
    """Одобрить HITL задачу и выполнить агента"""
    # Находим задачу
    task = None
    for t in hitl_tasks.values():
        if t.id == task_id:
            task = t
            break
    
    if not task:
        raise HTTPException(404, "Task not found")
    
    if task.status not in ["pending", "ready_for_review"]:
        raise HTTPException(400, f"Task already {task.status}")
    
    # Обновляем статус
    task.status = "approved"
    task.approved_by = approval.get("approved_by", "human")
    
    save_task({
        'id': task.id,
        'status': task.status,
        'approved_at': datetime.datetime.now().isoformat()
    })
    add_log(task.id, 'approved', {'by': task.approved_by})
    
    # Получаем ID агента
    agent_id = task.data.get("agent_id", "codecraft_1")
    
    # Карта агентов
    agent_map = {
        "codecraft_1": "/Users/kirillkravcov/UniversalCreativeHub/ai-farm/agents/codecraft/agent.py",
        "coder": "/Users/kirillkravcov/UniversalCreativeHub/ai-farm/agents/codecraft/agent.py",
        "coder_1": "/Users/kirillkravcov/UniversalCreativeHub/ai-farm/agents/codecraft/agent.py",
    }
    
    agent_path_str = agent_map.get(agent_id)
    if not agent_path_str or not Path(agent_path_str).exists():
        logger.error(f"Agent {agent_id} not found")
        task.status = "failed"
        task.result = {"error": f"Agent {agent_id} not found"}
        return {"status": "failed", "task_id": task_id, "error": "Agent not found"}
    
    # Динамический импорт агента
    import importlib.util
    spec = importlib.util.spec_from_file_location(f"{agent_id}_agent", agent_path_str)
    agent_module = importlib.util.module_from_spec(spec)
    spec.loader.exec_module(agent_module)
    
    # Находим класс агента
    agent_class = None
    for attr_name in dir(agent_module):
        if attr_name.endswith("Agent"):
            agent_class = getattr(agent_module, attr_name)
            break
    
    if not agent_class:
        task.status = "failed"
        task.result = {"error": "No Agent class found"}
        return {"status": "failed", "task_id": task_id, "error": "No Agent class"}
    
    # Выполняем агента
    agent = agent_class()
    payload = {
        "task": task.description,
        "target_file": task.data.get("target_file", ""),
        "existing_content": task.data.get("existing_content", "")
    }
    
    result = agent.execute(payload) if hasattr(agent, 'execute') else agent.execute(task.description, payload)
    
    # Обновляем статус
    task.status = "completed"
    task.result = result

import os
import sys
import uuid
import time
import json
from typing import Dict, Any

# Добавляем текущую директорию в PATH для импорта локального модуля
sys.path.insert(0, os.path.dirname(os.path.abspath(__file__)))

from llm_client_local import OllamaClient

class CodeCraftAgent:
    def __init__(self):
        self.llm = OllamaClient(model="qwen2.5-coder:7b")
        self.name = "codecraft_1"
        self.type = "coder"
    
    def execute(self, payload: Dict[str, Any]) -> Dict[str, Any]:
        """Выполнение задачи генерации кода.
        
        Batuta передает payload в формате:
        {
            "task": "задача",
            "target_file": "/path/to/file",
            "existing_content": "содержимое" (опционально)
        }
        """
        
        # Извлекаем параметры из payload
        task = payload.get("task", "")
        target_file = payload.get("target_file", "")
        existing_content = payload.get("existing_content", "")
        
        # Читаем существующий файл если указан
        if target_file and os.path.exists(target_file) and not existing_content:
            with open(target_file, 'r') as f:
                existing_content = f.read()
        
        # Генерируем код через LLM
        prompt = f"""
        Задача: {task}
        
        Целевой файл: {target_file if target_file else 'новый файл'}
        
        Требования:
        1. Если файл существует - модифицируй его, сохранив существующую структуру
        2. Добавь только необходимые изменения
        3. Выведи ПОЛНОСТЬЮ итоговый файл
        """

```bash
cd /Users/kirillkravcov/UniversalCreativeHub/tactical-command-v3

# 1. Обновим CreateTaskForm - заменим codecraft на codecraft_1
cat > src/features/hitl/components/CreateTaskForm.tsx << 'EOF'
import { useState } from 'react';
import { useMutation, useQueryClient } from '@tanstack/react-query';
import { hitlApi } from '../../../shared/api/batuta';
import { Send } from 'lucide-react';
import toast from 'react-hot-toast';

interface CreateTaskFormProps {
  onSuccess?: () => void;
}

export function CreateTaskForm({ onSuccess }: CreateTaskFormProps) {
  const [title, setTitle] = useState('');
  const [description, setDescription] = useState('');
  const [agentType, setAgentType] = useState('codecraft_1');
  const queryClient = useQueryClient();

  const createMutation = useMutation({
    mutationFn: (data: any) => hitlApi.createTask(data),
    onSuccess: () => {
      queryClient.invalidateQueries({ queryKey: ['hitl-tasks'] });
      toast.success('Task created successfully');
      setTitle('');
      setDescription('');
      onSuccess?.();
    },
    onError: (error: any) => {
      console.error('Create task error:', error);
      toast.error(`Failed to create task: ${error.message}`);
    },
  });

  const handleSubmit = (e: React.FormEvent) => {
    e.preventDefault();
    if (!title.trim() || !description.trim()) {
      toast.error('Please fill in all fields');
      return;
    }

    createMutation.mutate({
      id: `temp_${Date.now()}`,
      title,
      description,
      status: 'pending',
      assigned_to: agentType,
      created_at: new Date().toISOString(),
      data: { agent_id: agentType }
    });
  };

  return (
    <form onSubmit={handleSubmit} className="max-w-2xl bg-uch-secondary/20 rounded-lg p-6 border border-uch-secondary">
      <div className="mb-4">
        <label className="block text-sm font-medium text-uch-text-secondary mb-2">
          Task Title
        </label>
        <input
          type="text"
          value={title}
          onChange={(e) => setTitle(e.target.value)}
          placeholder="e.g., Create monitoring configuration"
          className="w-full px-3 py-2 bg-uch-primary border border-uch-secondary rounded-md text-uch-text placeholder-uch-text-secondary/50 focus:outline-none focus:border-uch-accent"
        />
      </div>

      <div className="mb-4">
        <label className="block text-sm font-medium text-uch-text-secondary mb-2">
          Description
        </label>
        <textarea
          value={description}
          onChange={(e) => setDescription(e.target.value)}
          placeholder="Describe what you want the agent to do..."
          rows={4}
          className="w-full px-3 py-2 bg-uch-primary border border-uch-secondary rounded-md text-uch-text placeholder-uch-text-secondary/50 focus:outline-none focus:border-uch-accent resize-none"
        />
      </div>

      <div className="mb-6">
        <label className="block text-sm font-medium text-uch-text-secondary mb-2">
          Agent Type
        </label>
        <select
          value={agentType}
          onChange={(e) => setAgentType(e.target.value)}
          className="w-full px-3 py-2 bg-uch-primary border border-uch-secondary rounded-md text-uch-text focus:outline-none focus:border-uch-accent"
        >
          <option value="codecraft_1">CodeCraft (Code Generation)</option>
          <option value="explorer_1">Explorer (Code Analysis)</option>
          <option value="architect_1">Architect (Design)</option>
          <option value="general_1">General</option>
        </select>
      </div>

      <button
        type="submit"
        disabled={createMutation.isPending}
        className="btn-primary flex items-center gap-2 disabled:opacity-50"
      >
        <Send size={16} />
        {createMutation.isPending ? 'Creating...' : 'Create Task'}
      </button>
    </form>
  );
}
EOF
```

## 🔧 КОМАНДА 34: ИСПРАВЛЕНИЕ ОТОБРАЖЕНИЯ КОДА В UI

**Исполнитель:** Вы

**Задача:** Исправить TaskCard чтобы показывать код из `result.pr.content`

**Действия:**

```bash
cd /Users/kirillkravcov/UniversalCreativeHub/tactical-command-v3

cat > src/features/hitl/components/TaskCard.tsx << 'EOF'
import { useState } from 'react';
import { Check, X, ChevronDown, ChevronUp, Code, FileText, Clock } from 'lucide-react';
import { formatDistanceToNow } from 'date-fns';

interface HITLTask {
  id: string;
  title: string;
  description: string;
  status: string;
  assigned_to: string;
  created_at: string;
  result?: {
    code?: string;
    config?: Record<string, any>;
    analysis?: Record<string, any>;
    pr?: {
      content?: string;
      target_file?: string;
      type?: string;
    };
    status?: string;
    error?: string;
  };
}

interface TaskCardProps {
  task: HITLTask;
  onApprove: (taskId: string) => void;
  isApproving: boolean;
}

export function TaskCard({ task, onApprove, isApproving }: TaskCardProps) {
  const [isExpanded, setIsExpanded] = useState(false);

  const timeAgo = formatDistanceToNow(new Date(task.created_at), { addSuffix: true });

  // Получаем код для отображения
  const getCodePreview = () => {
    if (task.result?.code) return task.result.code;
    if (task.result?.pr?.content) return task.result.pr.content;
    if (task.result?.config) return JSON.stringify(task.result.config, null, 2);
    if (task.result?.analysis) return JSON.stringify(task.result.analysis, null, 2);
    return null;
  };

  const codePreview = getCodePreview();
  const targetFile = task.result?.pr?.target_file;

  return (
    <div className="task-card">
      {/* Header */}
      <div className="flex items-start justify-between">
        <div className="flex-1">
          <div className="flex items-center gap-3 mb-2">
            <h3 className="task-title">{task.title}</h3>
            <span className={`status-badge status-${task.status.replace(/_/g, '-')}`}>
              {task.status.toUpperCase().replace(/_/g, ' ')}
            </span>
          </div>
          <p className="text-uch-text-secondary text-sm mb-2">{task.description}</p>
          <div className="flex items-center gap-4 text-xs text-uch-text-secondary/70">
            <span className="flex items-center gap-1">
              <Clock size={12} />
              {timeAgo}
            </span>
            <span>ID: {task.id}</span>
            <span>Agent: {task.assigned_to}</span>
            {targetFile && <span>Target: {targetFile}</span>}
          </div>
        </div>

        {/* Actions */}
        <div className="flex items-center gap-2">
          {task.status === 'ready_for_review' && (
            <button
              onClick={() => setIsExpanded(!isExpanded)}
              className="p-2 text-uch-text-secondary hover:text-uch-text transition-colors"
            >
              {isExpanded ? <ChevronUp size={18} /> : <ChevronDown size={18} />}
            </button>
          )}
        </div>
      </div>

      {/* Expanded content - только для ready_for_review */}
      {isExpanded && task.status === 'ready_for_review' && (
        <div className="mt-4 pt-4 border-t border-uch-secondary">
          {codePreview && (
            <div className="mb-4">
              <div className="flex items-center gap-2 text-sm text-uch-text-secondary mb-2">
                <Code size={14} />
                <span>Generated Content Preview</span>
              </div>
              <pre className="bg-uch-secondary/50 p-3 rounded text-xs overflow-x-auto max-h-64">
                {codePreview}
              </pre>
            </div>
          )}

          <div className="flex gap-2">
            <button
              onClick={() => onApprove(task.id)}
              disabled={isApproving}
              className="btn-primary flex items-center gap-2 disabled:opacity-50"
            >
              <Check size={16} />
              {isApproving ? 'Approving...' : 'Approve & Execute'}
            </button>
            <button className="btn-secondary flex items-center gap-2">
              <X size={16} />
              Reject
            </button>
          </div>
        </div>
      )}

      {/* Для completed задач показываем результат сразу */}
      {task.status === 'completed' && codePreview && (
        <div className="mt-4 pt-4 border-t border-uch-secondary">
          <div className="flex items-center gap-2 text-sm text-green-500 mb-2">
            <Check size={14} />
            <span>Completed - Code generated</span>
          </div>
          <pre className="bg-uch-secondary/50 p-3 rounded text-xs overflow-x-auto max-h-64">
            {codePreview}
          </pre>
        </div>
      )}

      {/* Для failed задач показываем ошибку */}
      {task.status === 'failed' && task.result?.error && (
        <div className="mt-4 pt-4 border-t border-uch-secondary">
          <div className="flex items-center gap-2 text-sm text-red-500 mb-2">
            <X size={14} />
            <span>Error</span>
          </div>
          <pre className="bg-red-500/10 p-3 rounded text-xs text-red-400">
            {task.result.error}
          </pre>
        </div>
      )}
    </div>
  );
}
EOF

echo "✅ TaskCard обновлен — теперь показывает код из pr.content"
```

**Обновите страницу в браузере.**

Теперь:
1. Completed задачи должны показывать сгенерированный код
2. Ready задачи раскрываются по клику на стрелку
3. Failed задачи показывают ошибку

Проверьте и напишите результат!

## 📋 ШАГ 2: ДОБАВЛЕНИЕ ВКЛАДКИ "ALL TASKS"

**Исполнитель:** Вы

**Задача:** Обновить HITLCenter для отображения всех задач

**Действия:**

```bash
cd /Users/kirillkravcov/UniversalCreativeHub/tactical-command-v3

# Заменить HITLCenter.tsx
cat > src/features/hitl/components/HITLCenter.tsx << 'EOF'
import { useQuery, useMutation, useQueryClient } from '@tanstack/react-query';
import { hitlApi } from '../../../shared/api/batuta';
import { TaskCard } from './TaskCard';
import { CreateTaskForm } from './CreateTaskForm';
import { TaskStats } from './TaskStats';
import { useState } from 'react';
import { RefreshCw, Plus, List, CheckCircle } from 'lucide-react';
import toast from 'react-hot-toast';

export function HITLCenter() {
  const [activeTab, setActiveTab] = useState<'ready' | 'all' | 'create'>('ready');
  const queryClient = useQueryClient();

  const { data: tasks, isLoading, refetch } = useQuery({
    queryKey: ['hitl-tasks'],
    queryFn: () => hitlApi.getTasks(),
    refetchInterval: 5000,
  });

  const approveMutation = useMutation({
    mutationFn: (taskId: string) => hitlApi.approveTask(taskId),
    onSuccess: () => {
      queryClient.invalidateQueries({ queryKey: ['hitl-tasks'] });
      toast.success('Task approved');
    },
    onError: (error: any) => {
      toast.error(`Failed: ${error.message}`);
    },
  });

  const handleApprove = (taskId: string) => approveMutation.mutate(taskId);
  const handleRefresh = () => {
    refetch();
    toast.success('Refreshed');
  };

  const readyTasks = tasks?.filter(t => t.status === 'ready_for_review') || [];
  const allTasks = tasks || [];

  return (
    <div>
      <div className="mb-6">
        <div className="flex items-center justify-between mb-4">
          <h1 className="text-2xl font-bold text-uch-text">HITL Center</h1>
          <button onClick={handleRefresh} className="btn-secondary flex items-center gap-2">
            <RefreshCw size={16} />
            Refresh
          </button>
        </div>
        <TaskStats />
      </div>

      <div className="border-b border-uch-secondary mb-6">
        <div className="flex gap-4">
          <TabButton active={activeTab === 'ready'} onClick={() => setActiveTab('ready')} icon={<List size={18} />} label="Ready" count={readyTasks.length} />
          <TabButton active={activeTab === 'all'} onClick={() => setActiveTab('all')} icon={<CheckCircle size={18} />} label="All Tasks" count={allTasks.length} />
          <TabButton active={activeTab === 'create'} onClick={() => setActiveTab('create')} icon={<Plus size={18} />} label="Create" />
        </div>
      </div>

      {activeTab === 'ready' && (
        <TaskList tasks={readyTasks} isLoading={isLoading} onApprove={handleApprove} isApproving={approveMutation.isPending} emptyMessage="No tasks ready for review" />
      )}

      {activeTab === 'all' && (
        <TaskList tasks={allTasks} isLoading={isLoading} onApprove={handleApprove} isApproving={approveMutation.isPending} emptyMessage="No tasks" showAll />
      )}

      {activeTab === 'create' && (
        <CreateTaskForm onSuccess={() => setActiveTab('ready')} />
      )}
    </div>
  );
}

function TabButton({ active, onClick, icon, label, count }: { active: boolean; onClick: () => void; icon: React.ReactNode; label: string; count?: number }) {
  return (
    <button onClick={onClick} className={`flex items-center gap-2 px-4 py-2 font-medium transition-colors ${active ? 'text-uch-accent border-b-2 border-uch-accent' : 'text-uch-text-secondary hover:text-uch-text'}`}>
      {icon}<span>{label}</span>
      {count !== undefined && <span className="ml-2 px-2 py-0.5 bg-uch-accent/20 text-uch-accent rounded-full text-xs">{count}</span>}
    </button>
  );
}

function TaskList({ tasks, isLoading, onApprove, isApproving, emptyMessage, showAll }: any) {
  if (isLoading) return <div className="text-center py-12"><div className="inline-block animate-spin rounded-full h-8 w-8 border-2 border-uch-accent border-t-transparent" /><p className="mt-4 text-uch-text-secondary">Loading...</p></div>;
  if (tasks.length === 0) return <div className="text-center py-12 bg-uch-secondary/20 rounded-lg"><List size={48} className="mx-auto text-uch-text-secondary mb-3" /><p className="text-uch-text-secondary">{emptyMessage}</p></div>;
  
  return (
    <div className="space-y-3">
      {tasks.map((task: any) => (
        <TaskCard key={task.id} task={task} onApprove={onApprove} isApproving={isApproving} />
      ))}
    </div>
  );
}
EOF
```

import os

def modify_file(file_path):
    try:
        # Проверяем, существует ли файл
        if os.path.exists(file_path):
            # Открываем файл на чтение
            with open(file_path, 'r') as file:
                content = file.readlines()
            
            # Модифицируем содержимое файла (например, добавляем комментарий)
            new_content = []
            for line in content:
                new_content.append(line.strip() + '  # Измененное содержание\n')
            
            # Открываем файл на запись и сохраняем измененное содержимое
            with open(file_path, 'w') as file:
                file.writelines(new_content)
            
            print(f"Файл {file_path} успешно модифицирован.")
        else:
            print(f"Файл {file_path} не существует. Создаем новый файл.")

## 📋 ШАГ 4: ДОБАВЛЕНИЕ КНОПКИ "PROCESS" ДЛЯ PENDING ЗАДАЧ

**Исполнитель:** Вы

**Задача:** Добавить возможность вручную перевести pending задачу в ready_for_review

**Действия:**

```bash
cd /Users/kirillkravcov/UniversalCreativeHub/tactical-command-v3

# Обновим TaskCard — добавим кнопку Process для pending
cat > src/features/hitl/components/TaskCard.tsx << 'EOF'
import { useState } from 'react';
import { Check, X, ChevronDown, ChevronUp, Code, FileText, Clock, Play } from 'lucide-react';
import { formatDistanceToNow } from 'date-fns';
import { useMutation, useQueryClient } from '@tanstack/react-query';
import { hitlApi } from '../../../shared/api/batuta';
import toast from 'react-hot-toast';

interface HITLTask {
  id: string;
  title: string;
  description: string;
  status: string;
  assigned_to: string;
  created_at: string;
  result?: {
    code?: string;
    config?: Record<string, any>;
    analysis?: Record<string, any>;
    pr?: {
      content?: string;
      target_file?: string;
      type?: string;
    };
    status?: string;
    error?: string;
  };
}

interface TaskCardProps {
  task: HITLTask;
  onApprove: (taskId: string) => void;
  isApproving: boolean;
}

export function TaskCard({ task, onApprove, isApproving }: TaskCardProps) {
  const [isExpanded, setIsExpanded] = useState(false);
  const queryClient = useQueryClient();

  const timeAgo = formatDistanceToNow(new Date(task.created_at), { addSuffix: true });

  // Мутация для перевода pending -> ready_for_review
  const processMutation = useMutation({
    mutationFn: async (taskId: string) => {
      // Имитация обработки агентом
      const response = await fetch(`/api/batuta/api/v1/hitl/tasks/${taskId}/process`, {
        method: 'POST',
        headers: { 'Content-Type': 'application/json' },
        body: JSON.stringify({ status: 'ready_for_review' })
      });
      return response.json();
    },
    onSuccess: () => {
      queryClient.invalidateQueries({ queryKey: ['hitl-tasks'] });
      toast.success('Task moved to Ready for Review');
    },
    onError: () => {
      toast.error('Failed to process task');
    }
  });

  const getCodePreview = () => {
    if (task.result?.code) return task.result.code;
    if (task.result?.pr?.content) return task.result.pr.content;
    if (task.result?.config) return JSON.stringify(task.result.config, null, 2);
    if (task.result?.analysis) return JSON.stringify(task.result.analysis, null, 2);
    return null;
  };

  const codePreview = getCodePreview();
  const targetFile = task.result?.pr?.target_file;

  const handleProcess = () => {
    processMutation.mutate(task.id);
  };

  return (
    <div className="task-card">
      <div className="flex items-start justify-between">
        <div className="flex-1">
          <div className="flex items-center gap-3 mb-2">
            <h3 className="task-title">{task.title}</h3>
            <span className={`status-badge status-${task.status.replace(/_/g, '-')}`}>
              {task.status.toUpperCase().replace(/_/g, ' ')}
            </span>
          </div>
          <p className="text-uch-text-secondary text-sm mb-2">{task.description}</p>
          <div className="flex items-center gap-4 text-xs text-uch-text-secondary/70">
            <span className="flex items-center gap-1"><Clock size={12} />{timeAgo}</span>
            <span>ID: {task.id}</span>
            <span>Agent: {task.assigned_to}</span>
            {targetFile && <span>Target: {targetFile}</span>}
          </div>
        </div>

        <div className="flex items-center gap-2">
          {task.status === 'ready_for_review' && (
            <button onClick={() => setIsExpanded(!isExpanded)} className="p-2 text-uch-text-secondary hover:text-uch-text transition-colors">
              {isExpanded ? <ChevronUp size={18} /> : <ChevronDown size={18} />}
            </button>
          )}
          {task.status === 'pending' && (
            <button onClick={() => setIsExpanded(!isExpanded)} className="p-2 text-uch-text-secondary hover:text-uch-text transition-colors">
              {isExpanded ? <ChevronUp size={18} /> : <ChevronDown size={18} />}
            </button>
          )}
        </div>
      </div>

      {/* Для pending задач - кнопка Process */}
      {isExpanded && task.status === 'pending' && (
        <div className="mt-4 pt-4 border-t border-uch-secondary">
          <p className="text-uch-text-secondary text-sm mb-3">This task is waiting for agent processing.</p>
          <button onClick={handleProcess} disabled={processMutation.isPending} className="btn-primary flex items-center gap-2">
            <Play size={16} />
            {processMutation.isPending ? 'Processing...' : 'Process Now (Simulate Agent)'}
          </button>
        </div>
      )}

      {/* Ready задача - превью и approve */}
      {isExpanded && task.status === 'ready_for_review' && (
        <div className="mt-4 pt-4 border-t border-uch-secondary">
          {codePreview && (
            <div className="mb-4">
              <div className="flex items-center gap-2 text-sm text-uch-text-secondary mb-2"><Code size={14} /><span>Generated Content Preview</span></div>
              <pre className="bg-uch-secondary/50 p-3 rounded text-xs overflow-x-auto max-h-64">{codePreview}</pre>
            </div>
          )}
          <div className="flex gap-2">
            <button onClick={() => onApprove(task.id)} disabled={isApproving} className="btn-primary flex items-center gap-2 disabled:opacity-50">
              <Check size={16} />{isApproving ? 'Approving...' : 'Approve & Execute'}
            </button>
            <button className="btn-secondary flex items-center gap-2"><X size={16} />Reject</button>
          </div>
        </div>
      )}

      {/* Completed */}
      {task.status === 'completed' && codePreview && (
        <div className="mt-4 pt-4 border-t border-uch-secondary">
          <div className="flex items-center gap-2 text-sm text-green-500 mb-2"><Check size={14} /><span>Completed - Code generated</span></div>
          <pre className="bg-uch-secondary/50 p-3 rounded text-xs overflow-x-auto max-h-64">{codePreview}</pre>
        </div>
      )}

      {/* Failed */}
      {task.status === 'failed' && task.result?.error && (
        <div className="mt-4 pt-4 border-t border-uch-secondary">
          <div className="flex items-center gap-2 text-sm text-red-500 mb-2"><X size={14} /><span>Error</span></div>
          <pre className="bg-red-500/10 p-3 rounded text-xs text-red-400">{task.result.error}</pre>
        </div>
      )}
    </div>
  );
}
EOF
```

Отличный вопрос. Разберем архитектуру.

---

## 📊 АРХИТЕКТУРА ОБРАБОТКИ ЗАДАЧ В UCH

### Статусы задач и их смысл:

| Статус | Значение | Как попадает | Что дальше |
|--------|----------|--------------|------------|
| **pending** | Ожидает обработки агентом | Создана через UI или Clipping | Агент должен взять задачу, обработать и перевести в `ready_for_review` |
| **ready_for_review** | Готова к проверке человеком | Агент обработал и создал превью | Человек нажимает Approve или Reject |
| **approved** | Одобрена, выполняется | После Approve | CodeCraft генерирует код |
| **completed** | Успешно выполнена | После генерации кода | Integrator применяет изменения |
| **failed** | Ошибка | При любой ошибке | Просмотр ошибки |

---

### Почему pending задачи не обрабатываются автоматически?

Сейчас **нет активного воркера** который бы:
1. Забирал pending задачи
2. Отправлял их агенту (CodeCraft/Explorer)
3. Получал результат
4. Переводил в `ready_for_review`

**Как это должно работать в идеале:**

```
Clipping (Cmd+Shift+2) → pending задача → NLP Router → агент → ready_for_review
                                    ↑
                              Этот шаг отсутствует
```

### Где должен быть обработчик?

В папке `ai-farm/agents/batuta/scheduler/` должен быть файл `task_processor.py`, который:
- Каждые N секунд проверяет pending задачи
- Назначает их свободным агентам
- Ждет результат
- Обновляет статус на `ready_for_review`

---

## 📋 ШАГ 5: СОЗДАНИЕ TASK PROCESSOR

**Исполнитель:** Вы

**Задача:** Создать воркер для автоматической обработки pending задач

**Действие 1: Создать файл task_processor.py**

```bash
cd /Users/kirillkravcov/UniversalCreativeHub/ai-farm/agents/batuta/scheduler

cat > task_processor.py << 'EOF'
#!/usr/bin/env python3
"""
Автоматический обработчик pending задач.
Запускается как фоновый процесс и обрабатывает задачи в статусе pending.
"""
import asyncio
import httpx
import sys
import os
from datetime import datetime
from pathlib import Path

# Добавляем родительскую директорию для импорта
sys.path.insert(0, str(Path(__file__).parent.parent))

BATUTA_URL = "http://localhost:8010"

class TaskProcessor:
    def __init__(self):
        self.running = True
        self.agent_map = {
            "codecraft": "codecraft_1",
            "codecraft_1": "codecraft_1",
            "explorer": "explorer_1",
            "explorer_1": "explorer_1",
            "architect": "architect_1",
            "architect_1": "architect_1",
            "general": "general_1",
            "general_1": "general_1",
        }
    
    async def get_pending_tasks(self):
        """Получить все pending задачи"""
        async with httpx.AsyncClient() as client:
            try:
                response = await client.get(f"{BATUTA_URL}/api/v1/hitl/tasks", timeout=5)
                if response.status_code == 200:
                    tasks = response.json()
                    return [t for t in tasks if t.get('status') == 'pending']
            except Exception as e:
                print(f"❌ Ошибка получения задач: {e}")
        return []
    
    async def process_task(self, task: dict):
        """Обработать одну задачу"""
        task_id = task['id']
        assigned_to = task.get('assigned_to', 'general')
        description = task.get('description', '')
        
        # Нормализуем ID агента
        agent_id = self.agent_map.get(assigned_to, assigned_to)
        
        print(f"🔄 Обработка задачи {task_id} агентом {agent_id}")
        
        # Имитация обработки - создаем простой результат
        result = {
            "pr": {
                "content": f"# Generated for: {description}\n# Agent: {agent_id}\n\ndef process():\n    print('Task processed: {description[:50]}...')\n    return True\n",
                "target_file": f"sandbox/generated_{task_id}.py",
                "type": "code_generation"
            },
            "status": "success",
            "agent": agent_id,
            "processed_at": datetime.now().isoformat()
        }
        
        # Обновляем задачу через API
        async with httpx.AsyncClient() as client:
            try:
                # Отправляем результат и меняем статус
                update_data = {
                    "status": "ready_for_review",
                    "result": result
                }
                
                # Используем PATCH если есть, иначе нужен другой эндпоинт
                response = await client.patch(
                    f"{BATUTA_URL}/api/v1/hitl/tasks/{task_id}",
                    json=update_data,
                    timeout=10
                )
                
                if response.status_code == 200:
                    print(f"✅ Задача {task_id} переведена в ready_for_review")
                    return True
                elif response.status_code == 405:
                    # PATCH не поддерживается - попробуем обновить напрямую
                    print(f"⚠️ PATCH не поддерживается, обновляем напрямую...")
                    return await self.update_task_direct(task_id, update_data)
                else:
                    print(f"❌ Ошибка обновления задачи {task_id}: {response.status_code}")
            except Exception as e:
                print(f"❌ Ошибка при обновлении {task_id}: {e}")
        
        return False
    
    async def update_task_direct(self, task_id: str, data: dict):
        """Прямое обновление задачи (если нет PATCH)"""
        # Этот метод нужно будет реализовать через импорт hitl_tasks
        try:
            # Добавляем путь к main.py
            sys.path.insert(0, str(Path(__file__).parent.parent))
            from main import hitl_tasks
            
            if task_id in hitl_tasks:
                task = hitl_tasks[task_id]
                task.status = data.get('status', task.status)
                if 'result' in data:
                    task.result = data['result']
                print(f"✅ Задача {task_id} обновлена напрямую")
                return True
        except Exception as e:
            print(f"❌ Ошибка прямого обновления: {e}")
        return False
    
    async def run_once(self):
        """Один цикл обработки"""
        tasks = await self.get_pending_tasks()
        
        if not tasks:
            return
        
        print(f"📋 Найдено pending задач: {len(tasks)}")
        
        for task in tasks:
            await self.process_task(task)
            await asyncio.sleep(1)  # Небольшая пауза между задачами
    
    async def run(self):
        """Основной цикл"""
        print("🚀 Task Processor запущен")
        print(f"📍 Batuta URL: {BATUTA_URL}")
        print("🔄 Проверка задач каждые 10 секунд...")
        
        while self.running:
            try:
                await self.run_once()
            except Exception as e:
                print(f"❌ Ошибка в цикле: {e}")
            
            await asyncio.sleep(10)  # Проверка каждые 10 секунд
    
    def stop(self):
        self.running = False

async def main():
    processor = TaskProcessor()
    try:
        await processor.run()
    except KeyboardInterrupt:
        print("\n🛑 Остановка Task Processor...")
        processor.stop()

if __name__ == "__main__":
    asyncio.run(main())
EOF

echo "✅ task_processor.py создан"
```

## 📋 ШАГ 6: ДОБАВЛЕНИЕ PATCH ЭНДПОИНТА В BATUTA

**Исполнитель:** Вы

**Задача:** Добавить эндпоинт для обновления статуса задачи

**Действие:**

```bash
cd /Users/kirillkravcov/UniversalCreativeHub/ai-farm/agents/batuta

# Добавим PATCH эндпоинт в main.py
cat >> main.py << 'EOF'

# ========== PATCH ЭНДПОИНТ ДЛЯ ОБНОВЛЕНИЯ ЗАДАЧИ ==========

@app.patch("/api/v1/hitl/tasks/{task_id}")
async def update_task_status(task_id: str, update: dict):
    """Обновить статус и результат задачи"""
    task = hitl_tasks.get(task_id)
    if not task:
        raise HTTPException(404, "Task not found")
    
    if "status" in update:
        task.status = update["status"]
    if "result" in update:
        task.result = update["result"]
    
    # Сохраняем в историю
    save_task({
        'id': task.id,
        'status': task.status,
        'completed_at': datetime.datetime.now().isoformat() if task.status == "completed" else None
    })
    add_log(task.id, 'status_updated', {'new_status': task.status})
    
    logger.info(f"Task {task_id} updated: status={task.status}")
    return task
EOF

echo "✅ PATCH эндпоинт добавлен"
```

## 📋 ШАГ 19: РЕДАКТИРОВАНИЕ MAIN.PY

**Исполнитель:** Вы

**Задача:** Добавить код загрузки задач в startup_event

```bash
cd /Users/kirillkravcov/UniversalCreativeHub/ai-farm/agents/batuta

# Создадим патч для добавления загрузки задач
cat > patch_startup.py << 'EOF'
import sys

with open('main.py', 'r') as f:
    lines = f.readlines()

# Ищем конец startup_event (где заканчивается регистрация агентов и сервисов)
insert_pos = None
for i, line in enumerate(lines):
    if 'services["graph_api"]' in line:
        insert_pos = i + 1
        break

if insert_pos:
    load_code = '''
    # Загружаем задачи из БД
    try:
        from task_history import load_all_tasks
        loaded_tasks = load_all_tasks()
        for task_data in loaded_tasks:
            task = HITLTask(**task_data)
            hitl_tasks[task.id] = task
        logger.info(f"Loaded {len(loaded_tasks)} tasks from database")
    except Exception as e:
        logger.warning(f"Could not load tasks from DB: {e}")
'''
    lines.insert(insert_pos, load_code)
    
    with open('main.py', 'w') as f:
        f.writelines(lines)
    
    print("✅ Код загрузки задач добавлен в startup_event")
else:
    print("❌ Не найдено место для вставки")
EOF

python patch_startup.py

# Проверим что добавилось
grep -A 5 "Загружаем задачи" main.py
```

```bash
def load_all_tasks() -> list:
    """Загружает все задачи из БД для восстановления при старте"""
    conn = get_db()
    cursor = conn.cursor()
    
    cursor.execute('''
        SELECT id, title, description, status, agent_id, created_at, 
               approved_at, completed_at, result, error
        FROM tasks
        WHERE status NOT IN ('completed', 'failed')
        ORDER BY created_at DESC
    ''')
    
    rows = cursor.fetchall()
    conn.close()
    
    tasks = []
    for row in rows:
        task = {
            'id': row['id'],
            'title': row['title'],
            'description': row['description'],
            'status': row['status'],
            'assigned_to': row['agent_id'],
            'created_at': row['created_at'],
            'approved_at': row['approved_at'],
            'completed_at': row['completed_at'],
            'result': json.loads(row['result']) if row['result'] else None,
            'error': row['error'],
            'data': {'agent_id': row['agent_id']}
        }
        tasks.append(task)
    
    return tasks
EOF
```
